# Post-Training

Everything after pre-training: instruction tuning, RLHF/RLAIF, preference optimization, and RL training methods that turn base models into assistants and reasoners.

### Surveys & overviews

Surveys and reviews of post-training, RLHF, and alignment.

| The KL Divergence Is Never Negative, Except in PyTorch | ★★★★☆ | [2611.30835](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30835) | Zhen Yang et al. | 2026-11-23 | 2026-11-24 | KL divergence is provably non-negative, yet PyTorch's formulas return negative values with large value and gradient errors for nearby distributions -- over 85% of FP32 values carry >=1% relative error near true KL 1e-7, and ~30% go negative at smaller divergences; more precision alone does not fix it. Stable closed-form decompositions, compensated arithmetic, and analytic gradients remove over 97% of significant errors. Downstream effects span parameter fitting, Bayesian decisions, identical-start distillation (up to 97% top-1 disagreement), KL-based layer rankings in a quantized LLM, and 19-59% of GPT-2 circuit edge scores.
